is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the offence is committed on a commercial basis or in a criminal organisation.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years if
a) as a result of the offence, a person who has not reached the age of eighteen obtains a prohibited performance-enhancing substance,
b) a person who has reached the age of eighteen commits the offence defined in point a) or b) of paragraph (1) by using a person who has not reached the age of eighteen.  
(4) Anyone who commits a preparatory act directed at the abuse of a performance-enhancing substance defined in paragraphs (1)–(3) is punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(5) A person who has reached the age of eighteen who induces a person who has not reached the age of eighteen to use a prohibited performance-enhancing substance, or provides assistance to such person for the use of a prohibited performance-enhancing substance, is punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(6) * For the purposes of this Section, a prohibited performance-enhancing substance means: any substance which, in view of its active ingredient, belongs to anabolic agents, peptide hormones, growth factors and related compounds, hormone antagonists and modulators, and is listed by name in the government decree promulgating the international convention against doping in sport or its amendment.  
185/A. § * (1) Anyone who
a) falsifies a medicinal product or veterinary medicinal product, or produces a counterfeit medicinal product or veterinary medicinal product,
b) places on the market or trades in a counterfeit, falsified or medicinal product or veterinary medicinal product not authorised in Hungary, or offers, transfers it in an unjustified quantity,
c) imports into the territory of the country, exports from it, transports through it, or acquires, possesses in an unjustified quantity a counterfeit or falsified medicinal product or veterinary medicinal product,
d) acquires, possesses in an unjustified quantity, imports into the territory of the country, exports from it, transports through it a medicinal product or veterinary medicinal product not authorised in Hungary,
e) uses an original document related to a medicinal product or veterinary medicinal product for commercial purposes in a manner contrary to its intended purpose,
is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) * If the offence defined in paragraph (1) is committed in respect of a medicinal product containing a psychotropic substance listed in Schedule 3 or 4 of psychotropic substances in Annex 2 to Decree No 78/2022 (XII. 28.) of the Minister of the Interior on controlled substances [hereinafter: Decree No 78/2022 (XII. 28.) BM], or in respect of the active ingredient of such a medicinal product in breach of the notification obligation, without authorisation or exceeding the limits of the authorisation, the punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(3) The punishment in the cases defined in points a) and b) of paragraph (1) is
a) imprisonment from one year to five years if the offence causes permanent disability or serious deterioration of health,
b) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offence causes death.  
(4) The punishment in the case defined in paragraph (2) is
a) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offence under point a) or b) of paragraph (1) causes permanent disability or serious deterioration of health,
b) imprisonment from five years to ten years if the offence under point a) or b) of paragraph (1) causes death.  
(5) Anyone who commits the offence
a) as a healthcare professional,
b) as an employee of an organisation authorised to manufacture, wholesale, or provide direct supply to the public, or
c) in a criminal organisation
is punishable in the case defined in paragraph (1) by imprisonment from one year to five years, and in the case defined in paragraph (2) by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(6) The punishment is as provided in paragraph (5) if the counterfeit, falsified or medicinal product or veterinary medicinal product not authorised in Hungary becomes widely accessible to users.  
(7) Anyone who commits a preparatory act directed at the falsification of medicinal products defined in paragraphs (1)–(6) is punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(8) For the purposes of this Section
a) an unjustified quantity means a quantity which can be established as not intended to satisfy the personal needs of a specific person,
b) a medicinal product or veterinary medicinal product not authorised in Hungary also means a product in which a pharmaceutical active ingredient is used in breach of the legal provisions on the composition of the product in question. A medicinal product not holding a marketing authorisation in Hungary must also be considered an authorised medicinal product if conduct defined in point b) or d) of paragraph (1) is carried out in respect of it which may be lawfully performed in possession of an official authorisation prescribed by law or following notification.

## 186. § (1) Anyone who

a) falsifies a medical device, or produces a counterfeit medical device,
b) offers, transfers, places on the market or trades in a counterfeit, falsified or medical device not authorised in Hungary,
c) imports into the territory of the country, exports from it, transports through it, or acquires, possesses in an unjustified quantity a counterfeit or falsified medical device,
d) acquires, possesses in an unjustified quantity, imports into the territory of the country, exports from it, transports through it a medical device not authorised in Hungary,
e) uses an original document related to a medical device for commercial purposes in a manner contrary to its intended purpose,
is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment in the cases defined in points a) and b) of paragraph (1) is
a) imprisonment from one year to five years if the offence causes permanent disability or serious deterioration of health,
b) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offence causes death.  
(3) Anyone who commits the offence defined in paragraph (1)
a) as a healthcare professional,
b) as an employee of an organisation authorised to manufacture, wholesale, or provide direct supply to the public, or
c) in a criminal organisation
is punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(4) The punishment is as provided in paragraph (3) if the counterfeit, falsified or medical device not authorised in Hungary becomes widely accessible to users.  
(4a) * Anyone who commits a preparatory act directed at the falsification of a medical device defined in paragraphs (1)–(4) is punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(5) For the purposes of this Section
a) * medical device means: a medical device, an in vitro diagnostic medical device and an investigational medicinal product;  
b) an unjustified quantity means a quantity which can be established as not intended to satisfy the personal needs of a specific person;  
c) * a medical device not authorised in Hungary also means a medical device placed on the market without carrying out the conformity assessment procedure.

## 187. § (1) * Whoever for consideration or regularly

a) medical practice,
b) *
c) activities falling within the scope of non-conventional healing and naturopathic procedures
unlawfully carries out, is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(1a) * Punishable according to paragraph (1) is whoever for consideration or regularly provides health services by unlawfully presenting himself or herself as a psychotherapist.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if quackery is committed by simulating authorization to practice medicine.  
(3) For the purposes of this Section, a person authorized to practice medicine is one who
a) has a general medical or dental degree obtained at a domestic university,
b) has a general medical or dental degree obtained at a foreign university and naturalized, provided with an equivalency clause,
c) is a foreign national who may carry out medical activity on the basis of a permit without naturalization of his or her degree,
provided that he or she is not subject to a prohibition from medical activity.  
(4) Expulsion may also be imposed on the perpetrator of quackery.

## 188. §

(1) Whoever unlawfully manufactures, possesses, uses or places poison on the market, or whoever fails to take measures prescribed to prevent the abusive use of poisons or to exclude the endangerment of other persons, if a more serious criminal offense is not committed, is punishable for a misdemeanor by confinement.  
(2) For the purposes of this Section, poison means a dangerous substance or mixture which, on the basis of its toxicological properties, in case of inhalation, ingestion or absorption through the skin, endangers human life or may cause serious injury or serious damage to health.

## 189. §

(1) Whoever manufactures or possesses for the purpose of placing on the market an article of common consumption that is harmful to health is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) Whoever places a harmful article of common consumption on the market is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the criminal offense is committed
a) with respect to a significant quantity or value of harmful articles of common consumption,
b) in a criminal association, or
c) as a business
.  
(4) Whoever commits the criminal offense defined in paragraph (2) by negligence is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(5) The perpetrator of the criminal offense defined in paragraphs (1) and (4) is not punishable if, as soon as he or she becomes aware of the harmful nature of the article of common consumption, he or she does everything to ensure that the harmful article of common consumption is returned to his or her possession or is not placed on the market.

## 190. § (1) Whoever deprives another of his or her personal liberty

a) by violence or by direct threat against life or bodily integrity, or
b) by placing him or her in a state incapable of defense or expression of will or by exploiting such a state
and makes his or her release dependent on the fulfillment of a demand, is punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to fifteen years if the kidnapping is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,
b) in a criminal association,
c) while armed,
d) while equipped with weapons, or
e) against an official person or a foreign official person, with regard to this capacity
.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to twenty years or life imprisonment if the kidnapping is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of fourteen,
b) causing particularly serious disadvantage, or
c) causing death
.  
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from ten years to twenty years or life imprisonment if the kidnapping also constitutes intentional homicide.  
(5) Whoever commits preparatory acts for kidnapping is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(6) The punishment of a person who voluntarily abandons the kidnapping – before serious consequences have resulted from it – may be reduced without limitation.

## 191. §

Whoever obtains credible knowledge that the commission of a kidnapping is being prepared and does not inform the person concerned or the authority thereof as soon as he or she can, if the kidnapping is attempted or committed, is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 192. § * (1) Whoever

a) sells, purchases, exchanges, transfers or receives another person as consideration, or
b) transports, accommodates, conceals or obtains for another person for the purpose of committing the act defined in point a),
is punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(2) Whoever for the purpose of obtaining regular advantage induces another person to perform work, carry out work-like activity, provide other services, or engage in unlawful conduct
a) by deception, by abusing a power or influence relationship related to the victim's upbringing, supervision, care, medical treatment or other power or influence relationship with the victim, or by exploiting the victim's vulnerable situation, or
b) by coercion by violence or threat,
is punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(3) Whoever for the purpose of committing the act defined in paragraph (2) recruits, transfers, receives, sells, purchases, exchanges, transfers or receives as consideration, obtains for another person, transports, accommodates, conceals another person, is punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(4) Whoever commits the criminal offense defined in paragraph (2) or (3) for the purpose of performing a sexual act or unlawful use of the human body is punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five years to ten years.  
(5) The punishment in the case of the criminal offense defined in paragraph (2) or (3) is imprisonment from five years to ten years, in the case of the criminal offense defined in paragraph (4) imprisonment from five years to fifteen years, if it is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,
b) with the maltreatment of the victim,
c) as an official person, using this capacity, or
d) against multiple persons
.  
(6) The punishment in the case of the criminal offense defined in paragraph (2) or (3) is imprisonment from five years to fifteen years, in the case of the criminal offense defined in paragraph (4) imprisonment from five years to twenty years or life imprisonment, if it is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of twelve, or
b) causing danger to life or particularly serious disadvantage
.  
(7) Whoever commits preparatory acts for trafficking in human beings and forced labor, in the case defined in paragraph (1) is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year, in the case defined in paragraph (2) or (3) for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years, in the case defined in paragraph (4) by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(8) Whoever
a) uses or exploits the work, work-like activity, other service or unlawful conduct of the victim of trafficking in human beings and forced labor defined in paragraph (2), is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years,
b) commits the criminal offense defined in point a) by using a sexual act or for the purpose of unlawful use of the human body, is punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years
.

## 193. § *

## 194. §

(1) Whoever deprives another of his or her personal liberty is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the violation of personal liberty is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,
b) from a base motive or for a base purpose,
c) with the maltreatment of the victim,
d) against a person incapable of defense,
e) while armed,
f) while equipped with weapons,
g) causing significant harm to interests, or
h) by simulating official proceedings
.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years if the criminal offense committed against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen also qualifies under point b) or c) of paragraph (2).

## 195. §

Anyone who compels another person by force or threat to do, not do or tolerate something, and thereby causes significant harm to their interests, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years, if no other criminal offence is committed.

## 196. §

(1) Anyone who compels another person to perform or tolerate a sexual act shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(2) Anyone who commits sexual coercion
a) against a person who has not attained the age of eighteen years,
b) against their relative, or a person under their education, supervision, care or medical treatment, or by abusing another power or influence relationship existing in relation to the victim
shall be punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years if the sexual coercion is committed against a person who has not attained the age of fourteen years.

## 197. §

(1) Anyone who commits sexual violence and shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years, who
a) commits sexual coercion by force or by direct threat against life or physical integrity,
b) exploits another person's state of incapacity to defend themselves or express their will for a sexual act.  
(2) Anyone who also commits sexual violence and shall be punishable by imprisonment from five years to ten years, who performs or causes to be performed a sexual act with a person who has not attained the age of twelve years.  
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years if the criminal offence defined in subsection (1) is committed
a) against a person who has not attained the age of eighteen years,
b) by the perpetrator against their relative, or a person under their education, supervision, care, medical treatment or otherwise under their power or influence, or
c) by several persons on the same occasion, with knowledge of each other's acts.  
(4) * Anyone shall be punishable by imprisonment from five years to twenty years,
a) who commits the criminal offence defined in subsection (2)
aa) in the manner defined in subsection (1),
ab) against the victim defined in subsection (3) point b), or
ac) in the manner defined in subsection (3) point c),
or
b) if the criminal offence defined in subsection (3) point a) also qualifies under subsection (3) point b) or c).  
(4a) * The punishment shall be imprisonment from ten years to twenty years if the criminal offence defined in subsection (4) point a) subpoint aa) also qualifies under subsection (4) point a) subpoint ab) or ac).  
(5) Anyone who provides conditions necessary for or facilitating the commission of sexual violence shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 198. §

(1) A person who has attained the age of eighteen years who performs a sexual act with a person who has not attained the age of fourteen years, or induces such person to perform a sexual act with another person, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(2) A person who has attained the age of eighteen years who attempts to induce a person who has not attained the age of fourteen years to perform a sexual act with them or with another person shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(3) If the victim is a relative of the perpetrator or is under their education, supervision, care or medical treatment, or if the perpetrator commits the sexual abuse by abusing another power or influence relationship existing in relation to the victim, the punishment shall be
a) in the case defined in subsection (1), imprisonment from two years to eight years,
b) in the case defined in subsection (2), imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(4) A person who has attained the age of eighteen years who performs a sexual act with a person who has attained the age of fourteen years but has not attained the age of eighteen years by abusing a power or influence relationship existing in relation to them shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 199. §

(1) Anyone who performs a sexual act with their lineal relative shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) Anyone who has sexual intercourse with their sibling shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(3) A descendant shall not be punishable for the criminal offence defined in subsection (1) if they had not attained the age of eighteen years at the time of committing the act.

## 200. §

(1) Anyone who procures another person for the performance of a sexual act for another person for the purpose of gain shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) * Anyone who commits procuring and shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years, who offers or solicits a person who has not attained the age of eighteen years for prostitution.  
(3) * The punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years if the procuring is committed as a business.  
(3a) * If the criminal offence defined in subsections (1)–(3) is committed as a special recidivist, the punishment shall be
a) in the case defined in subsection (1), imprisonment from one year to five years,
b) in the case defined in subsection (2), imprisonment from two years to eight years,
c) in the case defined in subsection (3), imprisonment from five years to ten years.  
(4) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years if the procuring defined in subsection (1) is committed
a) against a person who has not attained the age of eighteen years,
b) by the perpetrator against their relative, a person under their education, supervision or care, or by abusing another power or influence relationship existing in relation to the victim,
c) by deception, force or threat.  
(5) * The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years if
a) the procuring defined in subsection (4) point a) or b) also qualifies under point c),
b) the procuring defined in any of the points of subsection (4) is committed as a special recidivist.  
(6) Anyone who agrees to commit procuring as a business shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 201. § (1) Anyone who

a) induces another person to engage in prostitution,
b) makes a building or other place available to another person for the purpose of prostitution,
c) *
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) * Anyone who commits the criminal offence defined in subsection (1) in connection with the prostitution of a person who has not attained the age of eighteen years, or provides assistance to the prostitution of a person who has not attained the age of eighteen years, shall be punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(3) Anyone who maintains, manages a brothel, or provides material resources for its operation shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(4) * If the facilitation of prostitution is committed as a special recidivist, the punishment shall be
a) in the case defined in subsection (1), imprisonment from one year to five years,
b) in the case defined in subsection (2), imprisonment from five years to ten years,
c) in the case defined in subsection (3), imprisonment from two years to eight years.

## 202. §

Anyone who allows themselves to be wholly or partially supported by a person engaged in prostitution shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 203. §

(1) * Anyone who seeks to gain profit from the prostitution of a person who has not attained the age of eighteen years shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(2) * Anyone who provides consideration for a sexual act with a person who has not attained the age of eighteen years shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(3) * Anyone who allows themselves to be partially or wholly supported by a person engaged in prostitution who has not attained the age of eighteen years shall be punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(4) Anyone who maintains, manages a brothel, or provides material resources for the operation of a brothel in which a person who has not attained the age of eighteen years engages in prostitution shall be punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(5) * The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years if the exploitation of child prostitution is committed as a special recidivist.

## 204. §

* (1) Anyone who, in relation to a pornographic recording depicting a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,
a) acquires or possesses it, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years,
b) offers, transfers or makes it available, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years,
c) produces, places on the market, trades in it, or makes such recording available to the general public, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five years to ten years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), from five years to ten years in the case of point b), and from five years to fifteen years in the case of point c), if the offence defined therein is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of twelve,
b) against a person under the perpetrator's education, supervision, care or medical treatment, or by abusing another power or influence relationship in relation to the victim, or by exploiting the victim's vulnerable situation,
c) as an official person by using this capacity,
d) in relation to a recording containing maltreatment or the use of violence, or
e) as a special recidivist.  
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), from five years to fifteen years in the case of point b), and from five years to twenty years in the case of point c), if the offence defined therein is committed in relation to a recording containing maltreatment or the use of violence against a person who has not reached the age of twelve.  
(4) Anyone who
a) provides material resources for the offence defined in point c) of paragraph (1), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years,
b) commits preparatory acts for the offence defined in point c) of paragraph (1), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.  
(5) Anyone who, in relation to a pornographic recording depicting a person who has reached the age of fourteen but has not reached the age of eighteen,
a) acquires or possesses it, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years,
b) produces it, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years,
if none of the circumstances defined in points b) to e) of paragraph (2) exist.  
(6) Anyone who invites a person or persons who have not reached the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic recording shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(7) Anyone who invites a person or persons who have reached the age of fourteen but have not reached the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic recording shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years, if none of the circumstances defined in points b) to e) of paragraph (2) exist.  
(8) For the purposes of this Section, pornographic recording means: the depiction of another person or other persons with sexuality in a manner that is seriously offensive to decency, with openness, purposefully aimed at arousing sexual desire, including the realistic depiction of a non-existent person or persons.  
204/A. § * (1) Anyone who
a) participates in a pornographic performance in which a person or persons who have not reached the age of eighteen appear, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years,
b) features a person or persons who have not reached the age of eighteen in a pornographic performance or organises such a pornographic performance, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five years to ten years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), and from five years to fifteen years in the case of point b), if the offence defined therein is committed
a) against a person who has not reached the age of twelve,
b) against a person under the perpetrator's education, supervision, care or medical treatment, or by abusing another power or influence relationship in relation to the victim,
c) as an official person by using this capacity,
d) in relation to a performance presenting maltreatment or the use of violence, or
e) as a special recidivist.  
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to fifteen years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), and from five years to twenty years in the case of point b), if the offence defined therein is committed in relation to a performance presenting maltreatment or the use of violence against a person who has not reached the age of twelve.  
(4) Anyone who
a) provides material resources for the offence defined in point b) of paragraph (1), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years,
b) commits preparatory acts for the offence defined in point b) of paragraph (1), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.  
(5) Anyone who invites a person or persons who have not reached the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic performance shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(6) Anyone who invites a person or persons who have reached the age of fourteen but have not reached the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic performance shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years, if none of the circumstances defined in points b) to e) of paragraph (2) exist.  
(7) For the purposes of this Section, pornographic performance means: an act or presentation displaying the sexuality of another person or other persons with openness that is seriously offensive to decency, purposefully aimed at arousing sexual desire.

## 205. §

(1) Anyone who exhibits himself or herself in front of another person in a manner offensive to decency for the purpose of arousing or satisfying his or her sexual desire shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment up to two years.  
(2) A person who has reached the age of eighteen who engages in conduct offensive to decency in front of a person who has not reached the age of fourteen for the purpose of arousing or satisfying his or her sexual desire, if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.  
(3) If a more serious offence is not committed, anyone who engages in conduct offensive to decency towards another person which violates the victim's human dignity shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).

## 206. §

Expulsion may also be imposed on the perpetrator of exploitation of child prostitution, facilitation of prostitution, being kept and offence against decency.

## 207. §

The offence defined in paragraph (1) of Section 196, in point a) of paragraph (1) of Section 197, and in paragraph (3) of Section 205 is punishable only upon private motion, unless an offence not punishable upon private motion is also committed in connection with it.  
207/A. § * For the purposes of paragraph (3a) of Section 200, point b) of paragraph (5) of Section 200, paragraph (4) of Section 201 and paragraph (5) of Section 203, from the perspective of special recidivism, offences of a similar nature are trafficking in human beings and forced labour, procuring, facilitation of prostitution and exploitation of child prostitution.

## 208. §

(1) A person obliged to educate, supervise or care for a minor – including the cohabitant of a parent or guardian exercising parental supervision, as well as a parent deprived of parental supervisory rights if he or she lives in a common household or in one dwelling with the minor – who seriously violates his or her duty arising from this task and thereby endangers the physical, intellectual, moral or emotional development of the minor, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(2) If a more serious offence is not committed, a person who has reached the age of eighteen who
a) induces or attempts to induce a person who has not reached the age of eighteen to commit an offence or a petty offence, or to pursue a depraved lifestyle,
b) offers a person who has not reached the age of eighteen for the commission of an offence,
shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).

## 209. § Anyone who

a) violates the statutory provisions on the employment of persons who have not reached the age of eighteen, or
b) * employs a third-country national who has not reached the age of eighteen and does not have a permit issued for the purpose of work,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

## 210. §

(1) A person who, through their own fault, obstructs the establishment or maintenance of contact between a minor placed with them by official decision and a person entitled to contact with the minor, even after the imposition of a fine applied to enforce contact, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) * The offender shall not be punishable if they adequately ensure contact by the time the first-instance decision on the merits is delivered and commence the making up of missed forms of contact.

## 211. §

(1) A person who takes away a minor placed by enforceable official decision from the person with whom the authority placed them, without that person's consent, for the purpose of permanently changing the placement, or who keeps the minor hidden or in secret, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) A person who, in committing the offence defined in paragraph (1), uses violence or direct threat against life or bodily integrity at the time of taking away, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 212. §

(1) A person who, through their own fault, fails to fulfil their obligation to provide child support prescribed by law and in an enforceable official decision, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) A person who, through their own fault, fails to fulfil their obligation to provide maintenance prescribed by law and in an enforceable official decision, and thereby subjects the beneficiary to severe deprivation, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(3) * Under paragraph (1) the offender shall not be punishable, and in the case of paragraph (2) their punishment may be reduced without limitation, if they fulfil their obligation by the time the first-instance decision on the merits is delivered.  
212/A. § * (1) A person who, against their child's parent, and also against their relative, former spouse, former cohabiting partner, guardian, ward, legal guardian or ward who lives or lived in a common household or dwelling with them at the time of the offence or previously, regularly
a) engages in conduct that is seriously offensive to human dignity, humiliating and violent,
b) withholds material assets belonging to the common economy or common property and thereby subjects the victim to severe deprivation,
if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) A person who regularly commits against the person defined in paragraph (1)
a) bodily harm under Section 164(2) or defamation under Section 227(2), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years,
b) bodily harm under Section 164(3) and (4), violation of personal liberty or coercion under Section 194(1), by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(3) Barring order may also be imposed against the perpetrator of domestic violence.  
(4) The offence defined in paragraph (1) shall be punishable only upon private motion.

## 213. §

(1) A person who changes or terminates another person's family status shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years if the violation of family status is committed
a) by an employee of a healthcare provider or child welfare or child protection institution in the course of their occupation,
b) by a person obliged to raise, supervise or care for a person who has not reached the age of eighteen.  
(3) If an employee of a healthcare provider or child welfare or child protection institution commits the violation of family status negligently in the course of their occupation, they shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

## 214. §

A person who contracts a new marriage during the subsistence of their marriage, or who contracts marriage with a married person, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 215. § A person who

a) restricts another person in their freedom of conscience by violence or threat,
b) obstructs another person in the free exercise of their religion by violence or threat,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 216. §

(1) A person who, on account of another person's membership or perceived membership in a particular national, ethnic, racial, religious group or certain groups of the population, including in particular their disability, gender identity, sexual orientation, engages in provocatively anti-social conduct capable of causing alarm in a member of that group, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) A person who, on account of another person's membership or perceived membership in a particular national, ethnic, racial, religious group or certain groups of the population, including in particular their disability, gender identity, sexual orientation, ill-treats them or coerces them by violence or threat to do, not do or tolerate something, shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years if violence against a member of a community is committed
a) while armed,
b) while equipped with weapons,
c) causing significant harm to interests,
d) with the ill-treatment of the victim,
e) in a group, or
f) in a criminal conspiracy.  
(4) A person who commits preparation for violence against a member of a community shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

## 217. §

* (1) A person who obstructs another person in the exercise of their right of association or assembly by violence or threat shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) A person who obstructs, makes impossible or otherwise frustrates the conduct of an assembly, or seriously disrupts the assembly, if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
217/A. § * A person who, at an assembly, resists the organiser, leader or stewards of the assembly by violence or threat in the lawful performance of their stewarding duties, or ill-treats them during the performance of their stewarding duties, if another offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
217/B. § * (1) A person who violates the restrictions ensuring the peaceful character of the assembly under the Act on the right of assembly, if a more serious offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) The leader of the assembly shall be punishable under paragraph (1) if a steward employed by them violates the restrictions ensuring the peaceful character of the assembly and they do not take measures without delay to remove the steward from the time it comes to their knowledge.  
217/C. § * A person who organises a prohibited assembly or calls for participation in it under the Act on the right of assembly, if another offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

## 218. § (1) A person who performs

a) a medical intervention requiring consent or authorisation in connection with alteration of the human genome, alteration of the genome of an embryo, human reproduction or selection of the sex of a child to be born,
b) medical research that may be performed on a human being, embryo or gamete,
c) organ or tissue removal or organ or tissue transplantation performed for the purpose of transplantation
without the consent or authorisation of the person entitled, or omits the adequate information prescribed by law necessary for the exercise of the right to consent and authorisation, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) A person who removes an organ or tissue from a deceased person contrary to a declaration of objection shall be punishable under paragraph (1).  
(3) A person who negligently performs a medical intervention, medical research, organ or tissue removal or organ or tissue transplantation requiring consent or authorisation under paragraph (1) without the consent or authorisation of the person entitled shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

## 219. §

(1) * Anyone who, in violation of the provisions laid down in legislation on the protection or processing of personal data or in a binding legal act of the European Union, for the purpose of gaining profit or causing significant harm to interests,
a) processes personal data unlawfully or for a purpose other than intended, or
b) fails to take measures to ensure the security of data,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) * Anyone who, in violation of the provisions laid down in legislation on the protection or processing of personal data or in a binding legal act of the European Union, fails to fulfil their obligation to provide information necessary for the exercise of the data subject's right of access, and thereby significantly harms the interests of another person or persons, shall also be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1).  
(3) * The penalty shall be imprisonment not exceeding two years if the misuse of personal data is committed in relation to special data or criminal personal data.  
(4) The penalty shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the misuse of personal data is committed by an official person or through the use of a public mandate.

## 220. §

(1) Anyone who, in violation of the legislative provisions on the publicity of data of public interest,
a) conceals data of public interest from the person requesting the data, or fails to fulfil their obligation to provide information after the court has finally ordered the disclosure of the data of public interest,
b) makes data of public interest inaccessible or falsifies it, or
c) makes false or falsified data of public interest accessible or public,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) The penalty shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the misuse of data of public interest is committed for the purpose of unlawful gain.

## 221. §

(1) Anyone who enters another person's dwelling, other premises or the enclosed area belonging thereto by force, threat or simulation of official proceedings, or remains there, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) Anyone who enters another person's dwelling, other premises or the enclosed area belonging thereto, against the will of the person living there or having control over it, or by deception,
a) at night,
b) armed,
c) equipped with weapons, or
d) in a group,
or remains there, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(3) Anyone who prevents another person from entering their dwelling, other premises or the enclosed area belonging thereto,
a) if committed in the manner specified in paragraph (1), shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1),
b) if committed in the manner specified in paragraph (2), shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (2).  
(4) The penalty shall be imprisonment from one year to five years for a felony if the offense specified in paragraph (1) is committed in the manner specified in paragraph (2).

## 222. §

(1) Anyone who, for the purpose of intimidating another person or arbitrarily interfering with another person's private life or daily life, harasses them regularly or persistently, if a more serious criminal offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) Anyone who, for the purpose of causing fear,
a) threatens another person or their relative with the commission of a punishable act of violence against a person or causing public danger, or
b) creates the appearance that an event will occur that harms or directly endangers another person's life, physical integrity or health,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(3) * Anyone who commits harassment
a) against their spouse, former spouse, cohabitant or former cohabitant,
b) against a person under their education, supervision, care or medical treatment,
c) by abusing their position of power or influence, or
d) against an official person, in a place or at a time incompatible with their official activity,
shall be punishable in the case specified in paragraph (1) by imprisonment not exceeding two years, and in the case specified in paragraph (2) for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(4) * A person who has reached the age of eighteen and commits the harassment specified in paragraph (1) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

## 223. §

(1) Anyone who, without good cause, discloses a private secret that came to their knowledge by virtue of their occupation or public mandate shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by confinement.  
(2) The penalty shall be imprisonment not exceeding one year if the offense causes significant harm to interests.

## 224. § (1) Anyone who

a) destroys another person's closed consignment containing a communication, opens it for the purpose of learning its content, obtains it, or hands it over to an unauthorized person for such purpose, or
b) * intercepts a communication transmitted to another person via an electronic communications network – including an information system – if a more serious criminal offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by confinement.  
(2) The penalty shall be imprisonment not exceeding one year if the offense specified in paragraph (1) is committed through the use of an occupation or public mandate.  
(3) The penalty shall be
a) imprisonment not exceeding two years if the offense specified in paragraph (1),
b) imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the offense specified in paragraph (2)
causes significant harm to interests.

## 225. §

(1) Anyone who, by exploiting another person's vulnerable life situation, induces them to engage in self-degrading behavior, if a more serious criminal offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) The penalty shall be imprisonment not exceeding two years if the perpetrator
a) gives or promises consideration in the course of inducing the behavior specified in paragraph (1),
b) makes a recording of the person degrading themselves, or makes such a recording accessible to the general public.  
(3) The penalty shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the offense is committed on a commercial basis.

## 226. §

(1) Anyone who asserts or spreads a fact suitable for impairing the honor of someone in the presence of another person, or uses an expression directly referring to such a fact, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) The penalty shall be imprisonment not exceeding two years if the defamation is committed
a) from a base motive or for a base purpose,
b) before a large public, or
c) causing significant harm to interests.  
(3) * The act of a person who commits the acts specified in paragraph (1) through a press product or media service in the context of the free discussion of public affairs shall not be punishable for defamation, provided that their act is not directed at the manifest and seriously defamatory denial of the victim's human dignity.  
226/A. § * (1) Anyone who, for the purpose of impairing the honor of another person or persons, produces a false, falsified or untrue audio or video recording, if another criminal offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
226/B. § * (1) Anyone who, for the purpose of impairing the honor of another person or persons, makes a false, falsified or untrue audio or video recording accessible, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) * The penalty shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the offense is committed
a) before a large public, or
b) causing significant harm to interests.

## 227. §

(1) Anyone who, in addition to the acts specified in Section 226, uses an expression suitable for impairing honor against another person, or commits another such act,
a) in connection with the victim's performance of their job, fulfillment of their public mandate or public interest activity, or
b) before a large public,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) Anyone who commits the insult by assault shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1).  
(3) * The act of a person who commits the acts specified in paragraph (1) through a press product or media service in the context of the free discussion of public affairs shall not be punishable for insult, provided that their act is not directed at the manifest and seriously defamatory denial of the victim's human dignity.

## 228. §

Whoever desecrates a deceased person or their memory in the manner specified in § 226 or § 227 shall be punishable for a misdemeanor with the penalty specified therein.

## 229. §

(1) The perpetrator shall not be punishable for the offense specified in §§ 226–228 if the fact capable of impairing honor proves to be true.  
(2) Proof of truth is admissible if the assertion or spreading of the fact, or the use of an expression directly referring to it, was justified by the public interest or the legitimate interest of anyone.

## 230. § Against the perpetrator of harassment, restraining order may also be imposed

## 231. §

(1) The offense specified in § 218 shall be punishable only upon private motion, except if in connection with it an offense punishable otherwise than upon private motion is also committed. In the case of § 218 (2), the private motion may be filed by the person entitled to make a declaration as specified in the Act on Health Care, or by a relative or heir of the deceased.  
(2) The offense specified in §§ 221–228 shall be punishable only upon private motion, except if the offense specified in § 227 is committed against a member of a law enforcement agency.  
(3) In the case of § 228, the private motion may be filed by a relative or heir of the deceased.

## 232. §

(1) Whoever endangers the life or physical integrity of another or others by damaging or destroying a traffic route, vehicle, operational equipment or their accessories, by creating an obstacle, by removing or altering traffic signals, by deceptive signaling, by using violence or threat against the driver of a moving vehicle, or in another similar manner, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment shall be
a) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes serious bodily harm,
b) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass disaster,
c) imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the offense causes death,
d) imprisonment from five years to fifteen years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or fatal mass disaster.  
(3) Whoever commits preparation for the offense specified in paragraph (1) shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(4) Whoever commits the offense specified in paragraph (1) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year, and in the cases specified in paragraph (2), according to the distinctions made therein, by imprisonment not exceeding two years, three years, from one year to five years, or from two years to eight years, respectively.  
(5) The punishment may be reduced without limitation – in cases deserving special consideration it may be waived – against a person who voluntarily eliminates the danger before any harmful consequence has resulted therefrom.

## 233. §

(1) Whoever endangers the life or physical integrity of another or others by violating the rules of rail, air or water traffic shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment shall be
a) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes serious bodily harm,
b) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass disaster,
c) imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the offense causes death,
d) imprisonment from five years to fifteen years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or fatal mass disaster.  
(3) Whoever commits the offense specified in paragraph (1) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year, and in the cases specified in paragraph (2), according to the distinctions made therein, by imprisonment not exceeding two years, three years, from one year to five years, or from two years to eight years, respectively.  
(4) The punishment may be reduced without limitation – in cases deserving special consideration it may be waived – against a person who voluntarily eliminates the danger before any harmful consequence has resulted therefrom.

## 234. §

(1) Whoever exposes the life or physical integrity of another or others to direct danger on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic by violating the rules of road traffic shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment shall be
a) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes serious bodily harm,
b) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass disaster,
c) imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the offense causes death,
d) imprisonment from five years to fifteen years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or fatal mass disaster.

## 235. §

(1) Whoever by violating the rules of road traffic causes serious bodily harm to another or others by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) The punishment shall be
a) imprisonment not exceeding three years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass disaster,
b) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes death,
c) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or fatal mass disaster.

## 236. §

(1) Whoever operates a railway or aircraft, a mechanically propelled watercraft, or a mechanically propelled vehicle on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic while intoxicated shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) The punishment shall be for a felony
a) imprisonment not exceeding three years, if the offense causes serious bodily harm,
b) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass disaster,
c) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes death,
d) imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or fatal mass disaster.  
(3) Whoever causes the consequence specified in paragraph (2) by operating a non-mechanically propelled watercraft or a non-mechanically propelled vehicle on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic while intoxicated shall be punishable according to the distinctions made therein.

## 237. §

(1) Whoever operates a railway or aircraft, a mechanically propelled watercraft, or a mechanically propelled vehicle on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic under the influence of a substance having an adverse effect on driving ability, other than alcohol resulting from the consumption of alcoholic beverages, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) The punishment shall be for a felony
a) imprisonment not exceeding three years, if the offense causes serious bodily harm,
b) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass disaster,
c) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes death,
d) imprisonment from five years to ten years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or fatal mass disaster.  
(3) Whoever causes the consequence specified in paragraph (2) by operating a non-mechanically propelled watercraft or a non-mechanically propelled vehicle on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic under the influence of a substance having an adverse effect on driving ability, other than alcohol resulting from the consumption of alcoholic beverages, shall be punishable according to the distinctions made therein.

## 238. §

(1) A person who allows a railway or air vehicle, mechanically propelled floating installation, or a mechanically propelled vehicle on a public road or private road not closed to public traffic to be driven by a person in an intoxicated or drugged state, or by a person otherwise unfit to drive, is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) The punishment for a felony is
a) imprisonment not exceeding three years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious injury to health, or mass casualty,
b) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes death,
c) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or causes a fatal mass casualty.

## 239. §

If the driver of a vehicle involved in a traffic accident does not stop at the scene or leaves the scene before ascertaining whether anyone has been injured or requires assistance due to danger directly threatening their life or physical integrity, if a more serious offense is not committed, they are punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
239/A. § * (1) A person who drives on a public road or private road not closed to public traffic a road vehicle defined in the act on passenger transport services that has been modified without authorization or contrary to the authorization, or a vehicle providing special passenger transport services, if a more serious offense is not committed, is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding two years if the offense is committed with a school bus or a road vehicle serving the transport of persons with special needs – such as persons with reduced mobility, elderly or sick persons.  
(3) A person who modifies or has modified without authorization a vehicle defined in this § is punishable for a misdemeanor by confinement.  
239/B. § * (1) A person who drives a vehicle falling within the scope of validity of a driving ban while the driving ban is in effect is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) A person who commits the offense while a driving ban imposed in criminal proceedings is in effect is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 240. §

(1) The provisions established for offenses committed on public roads shall also apply where a violation of the rules on road vehicle driving causes at least serious bodily injury or death not on a public road.  
(2) For the purposes of §§ 233–235, provisions relating to pedestrians and passengers shall not be considered traffic rules.  
(3) * For the purposes of §§ 236 and 238, a person in an intoxicated state is a person whose body contains alcohol derived from the consumption of alcoholic beverages capable of producing a blood alcohol concentration exceeding 0.50 grams/liter or a breath alcohol concentration exceeding 0.25 milligrams/liter.

## 241. §

(1) A person who, by significant pollution or otherwise,
a) endangers,
b) damages to such an extent that its natural or previous state can only be restored by intervention,
c) damages to such an extent that its natural or previous state cannot be restored,
the land, air, water, wildlife, and their components, is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years in the case specified in point a), from one year to five years in the case specified in point b), from two years to eight years in the case specified in point c).  
(2) A person who commits environmental damage by negligence is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year in the case of subsection (1) point a), two years in the case of point b), three years in the case of point c).  
(3) * In the cases specified in subsection (1) point a) and in the first and second parts of subsection (2), the offender is not punishable, and in the case of subsection (1) point b) their punishment may be reduced without limitation, if by the time the first-instance decision on the merits is rendered they eliminate the danger or environmental damage caused by the offense and restore the original state of the damaged environment.  
(4) For the purposes of this §, pollution means: the loading of the land, air, water, wildlife, and their components exceeding the emission limit values established by law or in an administrative decision.

## 242. § (1) A person who

a) unlawfully acquires, keeps, places on the market, brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, transports through it, trades in, or damages or destroys a specimen of a strictly protected living organism,
b) specimens of a protected living organism or a plant or animal species of conservation importance in the European Union, provided that their combined value expressed in monetary terms as determined by separate legislation reaches the lowest monetary value established for specimens of strictly protected living organisms,
c) a specimen of a living organism falling under Annexes A and B of the EC Council Regulation on the protection of wild fauna and flora species by regulating trade in them,
is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the damage to nature causes destruction of specimens of living organisms to such an extent
a) that in the case of subsection (1) points a) or b) the combined value expressed in monetary terms as determined by separate legislation of the destroyed specimens of living organisms reaches twice the highest monetary value established for specimens of strictly protected living organisms,
b) which in the case of subsection (1) point c) endangers the survival of the population of the living organism.  
(2a) * A person who commits damage to nature by using poison or by placing bait capable of destroying an animal, endangering the life of several animals, is punishable according to subsection (2).  
(3) A person who commits the offense specified in subsection (2) by negligence is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(3a) * A person who commits preparation for the offense specified in subsection (2) or (2a) is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(4) For the purposes of this §, a specimen of a living organism means:
a) all developmental stages, forms, states of a specimen of a living organism,
b) a specimen resulting from crossbreeding and crossing of living organisms,
c) a derivative of a specimen of a living organism, which includes a dead organism, as well as any part thereof or of a specimen of a living organism, and also a product or preparation made from any of the foregoing or containing a component derived from any of them.

## 243. §

(1) A person who unlawfully significantly alters a Natura 2000 area, protected cave, protected natural area, or the community of protected living organisms or their habitat is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the damage to nature causes significant damage to or destruction of the Natura 2000 area, protected cave, protected natural area, or the community of protected living organisms or their habitat.  
(3) A person who commits the offense specified in subsection (2) by negligence is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(4) For the purposes of this §, Natura 2000 area means the concept defined in the act on the protection of nature.

## 244. § (1) Whoever

a) unjustifiably mistreats a vertebrate animal in such a manner, or unjustifiably applies such treatment to a vertebrate animal, which is capable of causing its permanent injury to health or death,
b) drives away, abandons or exposes his vertebrate animal or dangerous animal,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) * The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the animal cruelty is committed
a) causing particular suffering to the animal,
b) causing permanent injury to health or death of several animals,
c) before a large public,
d) against an animal specified in the act on the protection and care of animals, in respect of which a prohibition from keeping animals for pleasure or from keeping the animal species concerned applies, during the period of the prohibition, or
e) within two years following conviction for the offense of animal cruelty or organizing prohibited animal fights.  
(3) * The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years if the animal cruelty is committed
a) by the use of poison or by placing bait capable of destroying the animal, causing the death of several animals, or
b) as a special recidivist.  
(4) * Whoever commits the offense specified in paragraph (1) point a) for the purpose of profit-making in connection with breeding, against more than ten animals kept for pleasure, by neglecting the care of a good manager, shall be punishable according to paragraph (3).  
(5) * Whoever commits preparation for the offense specified in paragraph (3) point a) shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(6) * For the purposes of paragraph (3) point b), for the purposes of special recidivism, similar offenses are damage to nature causing the death of an animal, animal cruelty, organizing prohibited animal fights.

## 245. § Whoever

a) carries out activity aimed at killing or capturing game in a hunting area without entitlement to hunt, or in another's hunting area without permission as a hunter,
b) kills or captures an individual of a game species subject to a hunting prohibition period specified in separate legislation, extending to all individuals of the game species,
c) carries out activity aimed at killing or capturing game or strictly protected or protected vertebrate animals with prohibited hunting equipment specified in separate legislation, by prohibited hunting methods or in a sanctuary area,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 246. § Whoever

a) without authorization carries out activity aimed at catching fish with a fishing net or other fishing equipment – excluding angling,
b) carries out activity aimed at catching fish with prohibited equipment specified in separate legislation, by prohibited methods or in a sanctuary area,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

## 247. §

(1) Whoever organizes or holds an animal fight with the participation of vertebrate animals, organizes betting on such animal fight or places a bet, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(1a) * Whoever participates in an animal fight shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) Whoever acquires, keeps, breeds, trains, tames or trades vertebrate animals for the purpose of animal fighting shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(3) * If the organizing of prohibited animal fights is committed as a special recidivist, the punishment shall be
a) in the case specified in paragraph (1), imprisonment from one year to five years,
b) in the cases specified in paragraphs (1a) and (2), imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(4) * For the purposes of paragraph (3), for the purposes of special recidivism, similar offenses are damage to nature causing the death of an animal, animal cruelty, organizing prohibited animal fights, violation of obligations in connection with keeping a dangerous animal or dangerous dog.

## 248. § * (1) Whoever

a) carries out waste management activity without registration or notification, or without permission or exceeding the limits of the permission, or
b) carries out other unlawful activity with waste and it is capable of endangering human life, physical integrity, health, the earth, water, air or their components, or individuals of living organisms
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) Whoever places waste in a location not authorized by the authority for that purpose
a) capable of endangering human life, physical integrity, health, the earth, water, air or their components, or individuals of living organisms, or
b) in significant quantity
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years if the offense specified in paragraph (1) or (2) is committed
a) in respect of hazardous waste,
b) in respect of particularly significant quantity of waste, or
c) as a special recidivist.  
(4) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offense specified in paragraph (3) point a) also qualifies under paragraph (3) point b).  
(5) Whoever commits the offense by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment
a) in the cases specified in paragraphs (1) and (2), not exceeding one year,
b) in the case specified in paragraph (3), not exceeding two years,
c) in the case specified in paragraph (4), not exceeding three years.  
(6) For the purposes of this Section
a) for the purposes of special recidivism, similar offenses are environmental damage and damage to nature;  
b) waste management activity means waste management under the act on waste, with the provision that the concept of transport shall include import into the country, export from it, and transit through it;  
c) significant quantity of waste means waste exceeding 1000 kg or 10 cubic meters;  
d) particularly significant quantity of waste means ten times the significant quantity of waste.

## 249. §

(1) Whoever manufactures, uses, imports into the territory of the country, exports from it or places on the market an ozone-depleting substance or a product containing such substance shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) Whoever commits abuse of ozone-depleting substance by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

## 250. §

(1) Whoever, without permission or notification, or exceeding the limits of the permission, in respect of dangerous radioactive material
a) produces, stores, places, transports,
b) acquires, possesses, handles, places on the market, processes or otherwise uses, transfers to a person not entitled to keep it, disposes of, imports into the territory of the country, exports from it, or transports through it,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(2) Whoever commits the offense in a criminal organization shall be punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(3) Whoever commits preparation for abuse of radioactive material shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(4) * Whoever commits the offense specified in paragraph (1) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(5) For the purposes of this Section, dangerous radioactive material means: a naturally occurring or artificially produced material, one or more components of which emit ionizing radiation, and which is dangerous to human life and health, or to the living and non-living environment.

## 251. §

(1) Whoever operates a nuclear facility without permission or exceeding the limits of the permission shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(2) Whoever commits the offense specified in paragraph (1) in a criminal organization shall be punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(3) Whoever commits preparation for abuse in connection with the operation of a nuclear facility shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 252. §

(1) Whoever deceives the authority or person entitled to decide in order to obtain a permit necessary for the application of nuclear energy shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.  
(2) Whoever fails to fulfil their obligation to notify in connection with the application of nuclear energy shall be punishable by imprisonment up to three years.

## 253. §

Expulsion may also be imposed on the perpetrator of environmental damage, damage to nature, poaching, illegal fishing, violation of the rules of waste management and the organisation of prohibited animal fights.  
253/A. § * For the purposes of this chapter, poison means a hazardous substance or mixture which, on the basis of its toxicological properties, endangers the life of an animal or may cause particular suffering or permanent damage to the health of an animal if inhaled, swallowed or absorbed through the skin.

## 254. §

(1) Whoever commits an act directly aimed at changing the constitutional order of Hungary by force or by threatening with force shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five to twenty years or by life imprisonment.  
(2) Whoever commits preparation for the violent change of the constitutional order shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.  
(3) The person whose voluntary withdrawal results in the discontinuation of the commission of the offence, or who voluntarily prevents its continuation, shall not be punishable for the violent change of the constitutional order.

## 255. §

(1) Whoever establishes or leads an organisation whose purpose is to change the constitutional order of Hungary by force or by threatening with force shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five to ten years.  
(2) Whoever participates in the organisation defined in paragraph (1) shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.  
(3) The person whose voluntary withdrawal results in the discontinuation of the conspiracy, or who voluntarily prevents its continuation, shall not be punishable for conspiracy against the constitutional order.

## 256. §

(1) Whoever participates in a riot whose direct purpose is to prevent by force or by threatening with force, or to coerce into taking measures,
a) the National Assembly,
b) the President of the Republic,
c) * the Curia,
d) * the Government or
e) * the Constitutional Court
in the exercise of their powers defined in the Fundamental Law, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.  
(2) Whoever organises or leads the riot defined in paragraph (1) shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.  
(3) Whoever commits preparation for insurrection shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.  
(4) A participant who leaves the riot voluntarily or upon the call of the authority before the commencement of violent acts shall not be punishable for insurrection.

## 257. §

(1) Whoever, for the purpose of disrupting the constitutional order of Hungary, destroys, renders unusable or damages a public utility, a production, public transport or telecommunications facility or its equipment, a public building or structure, product stock, war material or other property of similarly important nature by its purpose, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment if the sabotage results in particularly serious disadvantage.  
(3) Whoever commits preparation for sabotage shall be punishable by imprisonment up to three years.

## 258. §

(1) A Hungarian citizen who establishes or maintains contact with a foreign government or foreign organisation for the purpose of violating the independence, territorial integrity or constitutional order of Hungary shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment if the treason is committed
a) causing serious disadvantage,
b) by using state service or official mandate,
c) in time of war or
d) by calling in or using foreign armed forces.  
(3) Whoever commits preparation for treason shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years, in time of war from two to eight years.

## 259. §

A Hungarian citizen who, by abusing their state service or official mandate, establishes or maintains contact with a foreign government or foreign organisation, and thereby endangers the independence, territorial integrity or constitutional order of Hungary, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years, in time of war from five to fifteen years.

## 260. §

(1) Whoever, in time of war, for the purpose of weakening the military strength of Hungary, enters into contact with the enemy, provides assistance to it or causes disadvantage to their own or allied armed forces, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from ten to twenty years or by life imprisonment.  
(2) Whoever commits preparation for supporting the enemy shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.

## 261. §
