349/A. § * (1) Whoever concludes an agreement as a result of which the result of a competition or match organised in the competition system of a sports federation or otherwise included in the competition calendar of the sports federation is not formed in accordance with the competition rules or the principles of fair play, if another criminal offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years if the criminal offence is committed in a criminal organisation or on a commercial basis.

## 350. §

* (1) Anyone who, during an election, referendum or European Citizens' Initiative falling under the scope of the Act on Electoral Procedure or the Act on the Initiation of Referendums, European Citizens' Initiatives and Referendum Procedure,
a) obtains endorsements in violation of the rules of the nomination procedure by means of violence, threat, deception or material benefit,
b) obtains signatures by means of violence, threat, deception or material benefit for the purpose of initiating a referendum or European Citizens' Initiative,
c) votes without entitlement,
d) signs without authorization or provides false data,
e) obstructs a person entitled to participate in the election, referendum, referendum initiative or European Citizens' Initiative, or seeks to influence them by means of violence, threat, deception or material benefit,
f) violates the secrecy of the election or referendum,
g) falsifies the result of the election, referendum, referendum initiative or European Citizens' Initiative,
h) accepts material benefit for their endorsement during the nomination procedure, or for their signature during the initiation of a referendum or European Citizens' Initiative,
i) makes their vote conditional on the provision of material benefit and accepts material benefit in this regard,
commits a felony and is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) *
350/A. § * A member, responsible person or executive officer of a nominating organization within the meaning of the Act on Electoral Procedure, as well as a candidate within the meaning of the Act on Electoral Procedure, who uses prohibited foreign support or a pecuniary advantage derived from an agreement concealing the origin of prohibited foreign support for the purpose of circumventing this prohibition, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.

## 351. §

(1) Anyone who participates in the leadership of an association dissolved by a court, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.  
(2) Anyone who participates in the operation of an association dissolved by a court in a manner capable of disturbing public order is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanour.  
(3) Anyone who provides conditions necessary for or facilitating the leadership or operation of an association dissolved by a court, or provides material resources for it, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

## 352. § Anyone who

a) organizes activities aimed at maintaining public safety or public order for which they are not authorized by law, or
b) organizes activities creating the appearance of maintaining public safety or public order,
is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanour.  
352/A. § * (1) Anyone who unlawfully enters the territory of Hungary protected by a facility ensuring the protection of the state border order through the facility is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the felony defined in paragraph (1) is committed
a) while armed,
b) while equipped with weapons,
c) as a participant in a riot.  
(3) Anyone who commits the felony defined in paragraph (1) as a participant in a riot while armed or equipped with weapons is punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years if the felony defined in paragraph (2) or (3) causes death.  
352/B. § * (1) Anyone who destroys or damages a facility or device ensuring the protection of the state border order, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, is punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years for a felony.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years if the felony defined in paragraph (1) is committed
a) while armed,
b) while equipped with weapons,
c) as a participant in a riot.  
(3) Anyone who commits the felony defined in paragraph (1) as a participant in a riot while armed or equipped with weapons is punishable by imprisonment from five years to ten years.  
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from ten years to twenty years if the felony defined in paragraph (2) or (3) causes death.  
352/C. § * Anyone who obstructs work related to the construction or maintenance of a facility ensuring the protection of the state border order, if another criminal offence is not committed, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year for a misdemeanour.

## 353. §

(1) * Anyone who provides assistance to another person for crossing the state border in violation of legal provisions is punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years for a felony.  
(2) * The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years if the smuggling of migrants is committed
a) for pecuniary gain,
b) by providing assistance to multiple persons for crossing the state border, or
c) by destroying or damaging a facility or device ensuring the protection of the state border order.  
(3) * The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years if the smuggling of migrants is committed
a) by mistreating the smuggled person,
b) while armed,
c) while equipped with weapons,
d) as a business, or
e) in a criminal organization.  
(4) * The punishment is imprisonment from five years to fifteen years if
a) the smuggling of migrants under paragraph (3) point a) is committed in the manner specified in points b)–e)
b) the smuggling of migrants under paragraph (3) point b) is committed in the manner specified in point a) or points c)–e).  
(5) * The organizer or director of the criminal offence defined in paragraph (3) or (4) is punishable by imprisonment from ten years to twenty years.  
(6) * Anyone who commits preparation for smuggling of migrants is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
353/A. § * (1) Anyone who participates in enabling another person to initiate asylum or other proceedings in Hungary allowing the acquisition of a residence title by making a false statement or concealing a true fact regarding a circumstance materially affecting the official procedure, or to make a false statement or conceal a true fact regarding a circumstance materially affecting the official procedure in such proceedings, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, is punishable by detention for a misdemeanour.  
(2) The organizer of the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1) is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(3) Anyone who commits the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1)
a) for pecuniary gain, or
b) by providing assistance to multiple persons
is punishable under paragraph (2).  
(4) Anyone who provides material resources for the commission of the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1) or paragraph (2) is punishable under paragraph (2).  
(5) Criminal proceedings for the criminal offences defined in paragraphs (1)–(4) may be initiated only on the basis of a complaint by the authority conducting the underlying case in which the criminal offences were committed, as long as that underlying case has not been concluded. Except for the case of such a complaint, the limitation period for the criminal offences defined in paragraphs (1)–(4) begins on the day the underlying case is concluded.  
(6) The perpetrator of the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1) is not punishable if
a) in the event of disclosure of the truth, the subject of the official procedure would accuse themselves or their relative of committing a criminal offence, or
b) they report the criminal offence to the authority and disclose the circumstances of its commission before it comes to the attention of the authority.  
(7) The punishment may be reduced without limitation – and in cases deserving special consideration may be waived – for the perpetrator of the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (2)–(4) if they report the criminal offence to the authority and disclose the circumstances of its commission before it comes to the attention of the authority.

## 354. § (1) Whoever

a) in the territory of a Member State of the European Union,
b) in another state party to the Agreement on the European Economic Area, or
c) in the territory of a state whose nationals have the same legal status as nationals of another state party to the Agreement on the European Economic Area
provides assistance for the purpose of obtaining pecuniary gain to a person who is not a national of any of these states for illegal residence, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) If a more serious criminal offence is not committed, whoever provides assistance for the purpose of obtaining pecuniary gain for the illegal residence in Hungary of a foreign person shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).

## 355. §

A person who has reached the age of eighteen who establishes a family relationship solely for the purpose of obtaining a document certifying the right of residence for the purpose of material gain, or consents to a declaration of acknowledgement with full legal effect establishing paternity, if a more serious criminal offence is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

## 356. § (1) Whoever employs *

a) a third-country national regularly or permanently, or
b) a significant number of third-country nationals simultaneously
who do not hold a permit issued for the purpose of work, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) Whoever
a) * employs a third-country national who does not hold a permit issued for the purpose of work under particularly exploitative working conditions,
b) *
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(3) For the purposes of this Section
a) * particularly exploitative working conditions shall mean the concepts defined in the Act on the general rules on the entry and residence of third-country nationals;  
b) significant number: at least five persons.

## 357. §

(1) Whoever damages a monument, an object belonging to the category of protected cultural property or an archaeological site located on his or her own property shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) Whoever
a) destroys a monument or an object belonging to the category of protected cultural property in his or her ownership,
b) causes irreparable damage to a monument as a result of which it loses its monumental character, or
c) causes irreparable damage to an object belonging to the category of protected cultural property or to an archaeological site,
shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years.  
(3) * Whoever commits the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1)–(2) in relation to a foreign protected cultural heritage element in his or her ownership shall be punishable according to paragraphs (1)–(2).

## 358. § (1) Whoever

a) alienates an object belonging to a protected collection or a piece of a protected ensemble of objects without prior consent prescribed by law,
b) fails to fulfil the obligation prescribed by law to notify of a change in the ownership of an object, collection or ensemble of objects belonging to the category of protected cultural property,
c) takes abroad an object, collection or ensemble of objects belonging to the category of protected cultural property without authorisation, or exceeds the limits of the export authorisation,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) Whoever takes abroad an object belonging to the category of cultural property and subject to export authorisation without authorisation, or exceeds the limits of the export authorisation, shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).

## 359. § (1) Whoever

a) keeps, breeds, alienates or brings into the territory of the country a dangerous animal without authorisation,
b) violates the safety regulations prescribed by law for the keeping of dangerous animals,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) Whoever
a) with regard to a dangerous dog
aa) breeds, brings into or takes out of the territory of the country, competes, or keeps without authorisation,
ab) alienates or acquires in violation of law, or
b) with regard to a dangerous dog
ba) violates the obligation prescribed by law for neutering, or
bb) violates the safety regulations prescribed by law for keeping,
shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).  
(3) Whoever keeps, trains a dangerous dog for the performance of guarding or protection tasks, or performs such tasks with a dangerous dog, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(4) For the purposes of this Section, a dangerous dog is a dog individually declared a dangerous dog by an official decision.

## 360. § * Whoever

a) regularly organises prohibited gambling,
b) makes premises or equipment available for the regular organisation of prohibited gambling, or
c) invites to participate in prohibited gambling before a large public,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 361. § Whoever *

a) violates the rules of epidemiological isolation, observation, quarantine or control ordered to prevent the introduction or spread of an infectious disease subject to quarantine obligation,
b) during an epidemic violates the rules of epidemiological isolation, observation, quarantine or control ordered,
c) violates the rules of plant health or animal epidemic measures ordered to prevent or eliminate the introduction and removal, as well as the spread of infectious animal diseases or plant quarantine pests,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by confinement.

## 362. § Whoever in the European Union

a) unlawfully brings into the territory of the country, stores, transports, places on the market or releases into the environment propagating material of a genetically modified plant variety not authorised,
b) unlawfully releases into the environment propagating material of a genetically modified plant variety authorised for purposes not extending to cultivation use,
c) violates the prohibition on import, production, storage, transport, placing on the market or use ordered as a safety measure for the duration of the safeguard procedure in respect of propagating material of a genetically modified plant variety authorised for cultivation purposes,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

## 363. §

(1) Whoever violates his or her obligation to cooperate as defined in the Act on the State Audit Office during an audit by the State Audit Office, and thereby obstructs the conduct of the audit, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) The head of the audited organisation shall be punishable according to paragraph (1) if, despite a written request, he or she does not send an action plan to the State Audit Office.  
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the act defined in paragraph (1) frustrates the conduct of the audit.

## 364. §

* Expulsion may also be imposed on the perpetrator of people smuggling, illegal crossing of the border barrier, damaging the border barrier, obstructing construction work related to the border barrier, facilitating illegal residence, facilitating and supporting illegal immigration, and organising prohibited gambling.

## 365. §

(1) Whoever takes away another's property from another person for the purpose of unlawful appropriation in such a way that for this purpose
a) applies violence against someone, or direct threat against life or bodily integrity, or
b) places them in an unconscious or defenseless state,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.  
(2) Robbery also occurs if a thief caught in the act applies violence or direct threat against life or bodily integrity for the purpose of retaining the property.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the robbery is committed
a) while armed,
b) while equipped with weapons,
c) in a group,
d) in a criminal association,
e) for significant value,
f) against an official person, foreign official person or person performing public duties, during their official proceedings or performance of their duties,
g) against a person with limited capacity to recognize or avert the crime due to their old age or disability.  
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years if the robbery is committed
a) for particularly high value or exceeding that,
b) for significant value in the manner specified in points a)–d) of paragraph (3),
c) against an official person, foreign official person or person performing public duties, during their official proceedings or performance of their duties, in the manner specified in points a)–d) of paragraph (3).  
(5) Whoever commits preparation for robbery shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to two years.

## 366. § (1) Whoever takes away another's property for the purpose of unlawful appropriation

a) by intoxicating them or inducing their stupefied state for this purpose,
b) takes it from a person who is under the effect of violence or direct threat against life or bodily integrity applied by them during the commission of another crime, or
c) takes it from a person who is defenseless or has limited capacity to recognize or avert the crime due to their old age or disability,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the plundering is committed
a) for significant value,
b) in a group,
c) in a criminal association.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the plundering is committed
a) for particularly high value or exceeding that,
b) for significant value, in a group or in a criminal association.

## 367. §

(1) Whoever, for the purpose of unlawful gain, compels another by violence or threat to do, not do or tolerate something, and thereby causes financial disadvantage, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the extortion is committed
a) in a criminal association,
b) with threat against life or bodily integrity, or other similarly serious threat,
c) as an official person using this capacity,
d) by simulating official mandate or capacity.

## 368. §

(1) Whoever, for the purpose of enforcing a lawful or allegedly lawful financial claim, compels another by violence or threat to do, not do or tolerate something, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the vigilantism is committed
a) while armed,
b) while equipped with weapons,
c) in a group,
d) against a defenseless person.  
(3) Vigilantism does not occur if the application of violence or threat is a permitted means of enforcing the claim.

## 369. §

For the purposes of this chapter, for the purposes of special recidivism, similar offenses are violent crimes against property and crimes against property.

## 370. §

* (1) Whoever takes away another's property from another person in order to unlawfully appropriate it commits theft.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment up to two years for a misdemeanor if
a) the theft is committed for minor value or
b) the theft committed for petty offense value is committed
ba) in a criminal association,
bb) habitually,
bc) with violence against property – including the removal of a device serving to prevent the appropriation of the property without causing damage to its substance, or rendering the device incapable of preventing the appropriation of the property –,
bd) by pickpocketing,
be) with the simultaneous taking of one or more public documents, private documents or cash substitute payment instruments,
bf) by entering a premises or enclosed area belonging to it by deception, or without the knowledge and consent of the entitled person or user,
bg) using a false or stolen key,
bh) against a victim sharing a dwelling or similar premises with the perpetrator, or
bi) by unlawful tree felling in a forest.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment up to three years for a felony if
a) the theft is committed for higher value,
b) the theft committed for minor value is committed
ba) in one of the manners specified in points ba)–be) of paragraph (2),
bb) * for an object belonging to protected cultural assets, archaeological find or movable cultural heritage element under foreign protection,
bc) for an object of religious veneration,
bd) for an object on a corpse, or for an object dedicated to the memory of the deceased in a cemetery or burial memorial site,
be) for precious metal, or
c) the theft is committed for petty offense or minor value at the scene of public danger.  
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if
a) the theft is committed for significant value, or
b) the theft committed for higher value is committed in one of the manners specified in points ba)–be) of paragraph (2) or at the scene of public danger.  
(5) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if
a) the theft is committed for particularly high value, or
b) the theft committed for significant value is committed in one of the manners specified in points ba)–be) of paragraph (2) or at the scene of public danger.  
(6) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if
a) the theft is committed for particularly significant value, or
b) the theft committed for particularly high value is committed in one of the manners specified in points ba)–be) of paragraph (2) or at the scene of public danger.

## 371. §

(1) Whoever causes damage by destroying or damaging another's property commits vandalism.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment up to one year for a misdemeanor if
a) the vandalism causes minor damage, or
b) the vandalism causing damage not exceeding the petty offense value threshold is committed
ba) by placing graffiti, or
bb) in a criminal association.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment up to three years for a felony if
a) the vandalism causes higher damage,
b) the perpetrator damages
ba) * an object belonging to protected cultural assets, a monument, archaeological site, archaeological find or cultural heritage element under foreign protection,
bb) * an object of religious veneration or a church, other place designated for religious practice,
bc) a burial place, burial memorial site, or an object dedicated to the memory of the deceased in a cemetery or burial memorial site.  
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if
a) the vandalism causes significant damage,
b) the perpetrator destroys an object, building or place specified in points ba)–bc) of paragraph (3),
c) the vandalism is committed using explosives or explosive devices.  
(5) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the vandalism causes particularly high damage.  
(6) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the vandalism causes particularly significant damage.  
(7) For the purposes of this §, graffiti means: a pictorial, graphic or textual surface coating created with spray paint, marker pen or any other surface-forming material, which is not necessary for the proper use of the property.


This excerpt sits under: A jogszabály mai napon ( 2024.02.14. ) hatályos állapota.

## 372. §

(1) A person who unlawfully misappropriates property of another entrusted to them, or disposes of it as their own, commits embezzlement.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor, if
a) the embezzlement is committed for a lesser value or
b) the embezzlement committed for a petty offense value is committed
ba) in a criminal association,
bb) at the scene of a public danger,
bc) on a commercial basis.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony, if
a) the embezzlement is committed for a greater value,
b) the embezzlement committed for a lesser value is committed in any of the ways specified in points ba)–bc) of paragraph (2), or
c) * the embezzlement is committed in respect of an object belonging to the category of protected cultural property, an archaeological find or a movable cultural heritage element under foreign protection.  
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years, if
a) the embezzlement is committed for a significant value,
b) the embezzlement committed for a greater value is committed in any of the ways specified in points ba)–bc) of paragraph (2), or
c) the embezzlement is committed to the detriment of a person with limited capacity to recognize or avert the offense due to their advanced age or disability.  
(5) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years, if
a) the embezzlement is committed for a particularly large value or
b) the embezzlement committed for a significant value is committed in any of the ways specified in points ba)–bc) of paragraph (2).  
(6) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years, if
a) the embezzlement is committed for a particularly significant value or
b) the embezzlement committed for a particularly large value is committed in any of the ways specified in points ba)–bc) of paragraph (2).

## 373. §

(1) A person who, for the purpose of unlawful gain, deceives another or maintains another in error, and thereby causes damage, commits fraud.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor, if
a) the fraud causes lesser damage, or
b) the fraud causing damage not exceeding the petty offense value threshold is committed
ba) in a criminal association,
bb) at the scene of a public danger,
bc) on a commercial basis,
bd) by simulating a charitable fundraising campaign.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony, if
a) the fraud causes greater damage, or
b) the fraud causing lesser damage is committed in any of the ways specified in points ba)–bc) of paragraph (2).  
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years, if
a) the fraud causes significant damage,
b) the fraud causing greater damage is committed in any of the ways specified in points ba)–bc) of paragraph (2), or
c) the fraud is committed to the detriment of a person with limited capacity to recognize or avert the offense due to their advanced age or disability.  
(5) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years, if
a) the fraud causes particularly large damage, or
b) the fraud causing significant damage is committed in any of the ways specified in points ba)–bc) of paragraph (2).  
(6) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years, if
a) the fraud causes particularly significant damage, or
b) the fraud causing particularly large damage is committed in any of the ways specified in points ba)–bc) of paragraph (2).  
(7) For the purposes of this §, damage shall also include the unpaid consideration for a service received.

## 374. §

(1) A person who, for the purpose of unlawful gain, conducts simulated economic activity, and thereby causes pecuniary disadvantage, commits economic fraud.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor, if the economic fraud causes lesser pecuniary disadvantage.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony, if
a) the economic fraud causes greater pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) the economic fraud causing lesser pecuniary disadvantage is committed
ba) in a criminal association,
bb) on a commercial basis.  
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years, if
a) the economic fraud causes significant pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) the economic fraud causing greater pecuniary disadvantage is committed in the manner specified in point ba) or bb) of paragraph (3).  
(5) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years, if
a) the economic fraud causes particularly large pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) the economic fraud causing significant pecuniary disadvantage is committed in the manner specified in point ba) or bb) of paragraph (3).  
(6) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years, if
a) the economic fraud causes particularly significant pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) the economic fraud causing particularly large pecuniary disadvantage is committed in the manner specified in point ba) or bb) of paragraph (3).

## 375. §

(1) A person who, for the purpose of unlawful gain, enters data into an information system, modifies, deletes or makes inaccessible data processed therein, or influences the operation of the information system by performing other operations, and thereby causes damage, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years, if
a) the fraud committed by using an information system causes significant damage, or
b) the fraud committed by using an information system causing greater damage is committed in a criminal association or on a commercial basis.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years, if
a) the fraud committed by using an information system causes particularly large damage, or
b) the fraud committed by using an information system causing significant damage is committed in a criminal association or on a commercial basis.  
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years, if
a) the fraud committed by using an information system causes particularly significant damage, or
b) the fraud committed by using an information system causing particularly large damage is committed in a criminal association or on a commercial basis.  
(5) A person who causes damage by using a false, falsified or unlawfully obtained electronic cash-substitute payment instrument or by accepting payment with such an instrument is punishable under paragraphs (1)–(4).  
(6) For the purposes of paragraph (5), an electronic cash-substitute payment instrument issued abroad shall be afforded the same protection as a cash-substitute payment instrument issued domestically.

## 376. §

(1) A person who has been entrusted with the management of property of another, and causes pecuniary disadvantage by breaching the obligation arising therefrom, commits breach of fiduciary duty.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor, if
a) the breach of fiduciary duty causes lesser pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) the breach of fiduciary duty causing pecuniary disadvantage not exceeding the petty offense value threshold is committed by a guardian or custodian in that capacity.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony, if
a) the breach of fiduciary duty causes greater pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) the breach of fiduciary duty causing lesser pecuniary disadvantage is committed by a guardian or custodian in that capacity.  
(4) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years, if
a) the breach of fiduciary duty causes significant pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) the breach of fiduciary duty causing greater pecuniary disadvantage is committed by a guardian or custodian in that capacity.  
(5) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years, if
a) the breach of fiduciary duty causes particularly large pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) the breach of fiduciary duty causing significant pecuniary disadvantage is committed by a guardian or custodian in that capacity.  
(6) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years, if
a) the breach of fiduciary duty causes particularly significant pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) the breach of fiduciary duty causing particularly large pecuniary disadvantage is committed by a guardian or custodian in that capacity.

## 377. §

(1) A person who has been entrusted with the management or supervision of property of another based on law, and causes pecuniary disadvantage by negligently breaching or neglecting the obligation arising therefrom, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years, if the negligent management causes particularly large or greater pecuniary disadvantage.

## 378. § (1) Whoever

a) misappropriates a found object belonging to another, or does not hand it over within eight days to the authority or to the person who lost it, or
b) misappropriates an object belonging to another that came into their possession accidentally or by mistake, or does not return it within eight days,
is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) * The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding two years if the misappropriation is committed in respect of an object belonging to the category of protected cultural property, an archaeological find, or a movable cultural heritage element under foreign protection.

## 379. § *

## 380. §

(1) Whoever takes from another a mechanically propelled vehicle belonging to another in order to use it unlawfully, or unlawfully uses such a vehicle so taken or such a vehicle entrusted to them, is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the offense is committed
a) with violence or with direct threat against life or bodily integrity,
b) in a criminal association.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offense defined in paragraph (2) point a) is committed while armed, in an armed state, or in a criminal association.

## 381. §

(1) Whoever, exploiting another person's situation of need, concludes an agreement containing consideration disproportionate to an especially high degree, which is capable of causing the person obligated by the agreement, a relative of the person obligated by the agreement living in a common household with them, or a person maintained by the person obligated by the agreement on the basis of a maintenance obligation to be exposed to serious or further serious deprivation through the performance of the agreement, is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the usury offense is committed in a criminal association or on a commercial basis.  
(3) Expulsion may also be imposed on the perpetrator of a usury offense.  
(4) The punishment of a person who reports the usury offense to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority and discloses the circumstances of the commission may be mitigated without limitation.

## 382. §

* For theft, vandalism, embezzlement, fraud, fraud committed by using an information system, breach of fiduciary duty, misappropriation, or unauthorized taking of a vehicle, the perpetrator is punishable only upon private motion if the victim is their relative. This provision is not applicable if the perpetrator is also the guardian or custodian of the victim.

## 383. § For the purposes of this Chapter

a) thing shall also mean electric and other economically exploitable energy, as well as a document embodying a proprietary right which in itself ensures – or in the case of a dematerialized security, to the holder of the securities account – disposal over the proprietary value or right certified therein,
b) object of religious worship shall also mean an object used for the performance of religious rites,
c) precious metal shall also mean an alloy of precious metal or material subject to metal trade licensing,
d) for the purposes of special recidivism, an offense of a similar nature is:
da) * a violent offense against property,
db) an offense against property,
dc) an offense against intellectual property.

## 384. § (1) Whoever

a) presents the intellectual creation of another as their own and thereby causes pecuniary disadvantage to the rightful owner,
b) abusing their position, office or membership in an economic organization, makes the exploitation of another person's intellectual creation or the enforcement of rights attached to the creation conditional upon being given a share in the fee therefor or in the profit or gain derived therefrom, or being presented as the rightful owner,
is punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) For the purposes of this Section, intellectual creation means:
a) a literary, scientific or artistic work subject to copyright protection,
b) a patentable invention,
c) a plant variety eligible for protection,
d) a utility model eligible for protection,
e) a design eligible for protection,
f) the topology of a microelectronic semiconductor product eligible for protection.

## 385. §

(1) Whoever violates the copyright or related right or rights of another or others existing under the law on copyright, causing pecuniary disadvantage, is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) A person who fails to pay the blank media levy or the reprographic levy due to the author or the holder of related rights in respect of private copying under the law on copyright is punishable in accordance with paragraph (1).  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the violation of copyright or related rights is committed causing greater pecuniary disadvantage.  
(4) If the violation of copyright or related rights
a) is committed causing significant pecuniary disadvantage, the punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years for a felony,
b) is committed causing particularly large pecuniary disadvantage, the punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years,
c) is committed causing particularly significant pecuniary disadvantage, the punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years.  
(5) A person who violates the copyright or related right or rights of another or others existing under the law on copyright by reproduction or by making available on demand does not commit the offense under paragraph (1), provided that the act does not serve, even indirectly, the purpose of gaining income.

## 386. §

(1) Whoever circumvents effective technological measures as defined in the law on copyright for the purpose of gain is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) A person is punishable in accordance with paragraph (1) who, for the purpose of circumventing effective technological measures as defined in the law on copyright,
a) manufactures, produces, transfers, makes available, or places on the market a device, product, computer program, equipment or apparatus necessary for this purpose,
b) places at the disposal of another person economic, technical or organizational knowledge necessary for or facilitating this purpose.  
(3) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the circumvention of technological measures is committed on a commercial basis.  
(4) A person who, before their activity comes to the knowledge of the authority, discloses it to the authority and hands over the manufactured or produced thing to the authority and makes it possible to establish the identity of another person participating in the manufacture or production, is not punishable for the manufacture or production of a device, product, equipment or apparatus necessary for the circumvention of effective technological measures as defined in the law on copyright.

## 387. § Whoever for the purpose of gain

a) creates false rights management information,
b) removes or alters rights management information as defined in the law on copyright,
is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

## 388. §

(1) Whoever violates the right of the entitled person arising from industrial property protection existing under law, international treaty promulgated by law or European Union legal act by
a) imitating or copying the subject matter of the protection,
b) placing on the market goods produced by imitating or copying the subject matter of the protection, or acquiring or holding such goods for the purpose of placing them on the market
and thereby causes pecuniary disadvantage, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years for a felony if the infringement of industrial property rights is committed on a commercial basis.  
(3) If the infringement of industrial property rights is committed
a) causing significant pecuniary disadvantage, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years,
b) causing particularly large pecuniary disadvantage, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years,
c) causing particularly significant pecuniary disadvantage, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years.  
(4) For the purposes of this §
a) industrial property protection means:
aa) patent protection,
ab) plant variety protection,
ac) supplementary protection certificate,
ad) trademark protection,
ae) geographical indication protection,
af) design protection,
ag) *
ah) *
b) goods shall mean all tangible marketable movable things and services.  
388/A. § * In cases under Article 1(1) of Regulation (EU) No 608/2013 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 12 June 2013 concerning customs enforcement of intellectual property rights and repealing Council Regulation (EC) No 1383/2003, the offenses defined in § 385 and § 388 shall be punishable only upon private motion, unless an offense punishable otherwise than upon private motion is also committed in connection therewith.  
388/B. § * For the purposes of this Chapter, a property offense shall be considered an offense of a similar nature for the purposes of special recidivism.

## 389. § (1) Whoever

a) counterfeits or falsifies money for the purpose of placing it in circulation,
b) acquires, brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, transports through it false or falsified money for the purpose of placing it in circulation,
c) places false or falsified money in circulation,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two years to eight years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to fifteen years if counterfeiting of money is committed
a) in respect of money of particularly large or greater value, or
b) in a criminal organization.  
(3) Whoever commits preparation for counterfeiting of money shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(4) The punishment of a person who places in circulation false or falsified money of minor or lesser value lawfully acquired as genuine may be reduced without limitation.  
(5) For the purposes of this §
a) money means: banknotes and coins that qualify as legal tender, or will so qualify in the future, from a specified date, under law, European Union legal act or official announcement of the institution authorized to issue money, as well as banknotes and coins withdrawn from circulation if the national bank of the state that issued the withdrawn banknote or coin is obliged, or has undertaken an obligation, to exchange it for legal tender under law or European Union legal act,
b) serially issued securities produced by printing shall be subject to the same assessment as banknotes, provided that their transfer is not restricted or excluded by law or by a statement entered on the security,
c) counterfeiting of money shall also include such alteration of money withdrawn from circulation as to give it the appearance of money in circulation,
d) falsification of money shall also include the application or removal of a marking that serves to indicate that the money is valid only in a specific country, as well as the reduction of the precious metal content of money.  
(6) Foreign money and securities shall enjoy the same protection as domestic ones.

## 390. §

(1) Whoever prepares, transfers, accepts, acquires, holds, brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, transports through it, or places in circulation material, tool, equipment, production plan, technical description or computer program necessary for counterfeiting money shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if facilitating counterfeiting of money is committed in a criminal organization or on a commercial basis.

## 391. § (1) Whoever for the purpose of placing in circulation or use

a) counterfeits or falsifies a stamp,
b) acquires, brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, or transports through it a false or falsified stamp,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) A person who places in circulation or uses a false, falsified or used stamp as genuine or unused shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).  
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years if counterfeiting of stamps is committed
a) in respect of a stamp of particularly large or greater value, or
b) in a criminal organization.  
(4) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding one year for a misdemeanor if counterfeiting of stamps is committed in respect of a stamp of minor or lesser value.  
(5) For the purposes of this §
a) stamp means:
aa) postage stamp, even if it has not yet been placed in circulation or has already been withdrawn from it,
ab) marking suitable for franking postal services and the imprint of a franking machine, postal overprint, inscription or marking related to payment, and international reply coupon,
ac) stamp issued by an authority for the fulfillment of a payment obligation, even if it has not yet been placed in circulation or has already been withdrawn from it, until such time as the state is obliged under law to redeem or exchange it,
ad) official mark serving to certify the nature or content of metal,
ae) official stamp serving to secure tax, as well as official stamp serving to certify the quality, quantity or other essential property of a product,
af) stamp and seal used by the metrological authority to certify the calibration and verification of measuring instruments, as well as to mark the capacity of barrels,
b) placing in circulation shall also include placing in circulation for the purpose of stamp collecting,
c) falsification shall also include unauthorized alteration of a stamp serving the purpose of collecting.  
(6) Foreign stamps shall enjoy the same protection as domestic ones.

## 392. § (1) Whoever for the purpose of use

a) falsifies a cash-substitute payment instrument,
b) produces a false cash-substitute payment instrument, or
c) * records by technical means the data stored on an electronic cash-substitute payment instrument or the security elements associated with it,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) Whoever commits preparation for falsification of a cash-substitute payment instrument shall be punishable by confinement.  
(3) Cash-substitute or electronic cash-substitute payment instruments issued abroad shall enjoy the same protection as cash-substitute payment instruments issued domestically.

## 393. § (1) * Whoever

a) unlawfully takes away or acquires from another, without their consent, one or more cash-substitute payment instruments that are not or not exclusively their own, or to the use of which they are not or not exclusively entitled,
b) transfers, acquires, brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, or transports through it a false or falsified cash-substitute payment instrument taken away or acquired in the manner specified in point a), or the data stored on an electronic cash-substitute payment instrument or the security elements associated with it,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if abuse of cash-substitute payment instruments is committed in a criminal organization or on a commercial basis.  
(3) Cash-substitute payment instruments issued abroad shall enjoy the same protection as cash-substitute payment instruments issued domestically.

## 394. §

(1) * Whoever produces, acquires, possesses, transfers, places into circulation, brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, or transports through it material, equipment, device or computer program necessary for the counterfeiting of cash substitute payment instruments or for the technical recording of data on cash substitute payment instruments, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) * The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the offense defined in paragraph (1) is committed in a criminal association or on a commercial basis.

## 395. §

(1) Whoever misleads another person, keeps another person in error, or conceals the true facts for the purpose of obtaining or retaining social security benefits or pecuniary or benefits in kind provided to natural persons from the subsystems of the public finances on the basis of law, and thereby causes damage, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) * The punishment of a person who makes restitution for the damage caused by abuse of social security, social welfare or other welfare benefits before the indictment is filed may be reduced without limitation.

## 396. § (1) * Whoever

a) misleads another person, keeps another person in error, makes a false statement, or conceals the true facts in relation to an obligation to make payment into the budget or funds originating from the budget,
b) unlawfully takes advantage of a benefit in connection with an obligation to make payment into the budget, or
c) uses funds originating from the budget for a purpose other than the approved purpose,
and thereby causes pecuniary disadvantage to one or more budgets, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(2) *
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years if
a) the budgetary fraud causes significant pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) * the budgetary fraud defined in paragraph (1) is committed in a criminal association or on a commercial basis.
(4) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years if
a) the budgetary fraud causes particularly large pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) the budgetary fraud causing significant pecuniary disadvantage is committed in a criminal association or on a commercial basis.
(5) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five years to ten years if
a) the budgetary fraud causes particularly significant pecuniary disadvantage, or
b) the budgetary fraud causing particularly large pecuniary disadvantage is committed in a criminal association or on a commercial basis.
(6) * A person who produces, acquires, possesses, places into circulation, or trades in excise products in the absence of the conditions laid down in the law on excise duty and in the legislation based on its authorization or without official authorization, and thereby causes pecuniary disadvantage to the budget, shall be punishable under paragraphs (1)–(5).
(7) Whoever fails to comply or incompletely complies with the accounting, reporting or prescribed information obligation in connection with funds originating from the budget, makes a false statement, or uses a false or forged document, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.
(8) * The punishment of a person who makes restitution for the pecuniary disadvantage caused by budgetary fraud defined in paragraphs (1)–(6) before the indictment is filed may be reduced without limitation. This provision shall not apply if the offense is committed in a criminal association, in a criminal organization or as a special recidivist.
(9) For the purposes of this Section
a) budget shall mean the budget of the subsystems of the public finances – including the budget of the financial funds of social security and the separated state funds –, the budget managed by or on behalf of an international organization, and the budget and financial funds managed by or on behalf of the European Union. In respect of an offense committed in relation to funds originating from the budget, budget shall also mean the budget and financial funds managed by or on behalf of a foreign state, in addition to those listed;
b) pecuniary disadvantage shall also mean the loss of revenue resulting from the failure to fulfill the obligation to make payment into the budget, as well as funds unlawfully claimed from the budget or used for a purpose other than the approved purpose;
c) * for the purposes of special recidivism, an offense of a similar nature shall be an offense damaging the budget.

## 397. §

The manager, member or employee authorized to control or supervise of an economic organization, if he or she fails to fulfill the supervisory or control obligation and thereby enables a member or employee of the economic organization to commit budgetary fraud in the course of the activities of the economic organization, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 398. § (1) * Whoever

a) produces, acquires, possesses, places into circulation equipment, apparatus, device or raw material suitable for the production of excise products, as defined in the law on excise duty and in the legislation based on its authorization, without authorization or in violation of law, or
b) produces, acquires or possesses the closure mark necessary for placing into circulation without authorization or in violation of law,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony if the offense is committed
a) on a commercial basis,
b) in respect of a significant quantity of raw material or closure mark,
c) in respect of a tax stamp of significant or greater value.
(3) For the purposes of point b) of paragraph (2)
a) the raw material shall be of significant quantity if it exceeds
aa) 20,000 liters in the case of mineral oil products without tax rate that can be used as motor gasoline or gas oil, as motor gasoline or gas oil additive, or as diluent,
ab) 45,000 kilograms in the case of liquid hydrocarbons without tax rate,
ac) 100,000 nm3 in the case of gaseous hydrocarbons without tax rate,
ad) 10,000 liters in the case of sugar wash,
ae) 25,000 liters in the case of wash made from sugar or starch-containing agricultural products,
af) * 5 kilograms in the case of dried tobacco, fermented tobacco or cut tobacco,
b) the closure mark shall be of significant quantity if it exceeds 5,000 pieces.

## 399. §

* (1) Anyone who conceals or disguises the origin of property derived from a punishable act, the right over the property, the location of the property, or changes thereof, commits money laundering.
(2) Anyone who receives the property derived from a punishable act from another person, conceals it, transforms it, transfers it, participates in its alienation, uses it, conducts financial activity in connection with it, uses financial services, or disposes of it for the purpose of concealing or disguising the origin of the property derived from a punishable act, the right over the property, the location of the property, or changes thereof, also commits money laundering.
(3) Anyone who, by receiving from another person property derived from a punishable act, concealing it, transforming it, transferring it, participating in its alienation, using it, conducting financial activity in connection with it, using financial services, or disposing of it,
a) participates in frustrating the confiscation of property or asset recovery against another person, or
b) attempts to frustrate the confiscation of property or asset recovery against another person,
also commits money laundering.
(4) Anyone who
a) acquires property derived from a punishable act committed by another person, acquires the right of disposal over it, or
b) preserves, conceals, manages, uses, utilizes, transforms, transfers it, or participates in its alienation,
also commits money laundering.
(5) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding five years for a felony if the money laundering is committed for a value not exceeding significant value.
(6) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the money laundering is committed
a) for a particularly large value, or
b) for a significant value
ba) on a commercial basis,
bb) as a service provider, its officer or employee defined in the act on the prevention and combating of money laundering and terrorist financing, in connection with the service provider's activity, or
bc) as an official person.
(7) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the money laundering is committed
a) for a particularly significant value, or
b) for a particularly large value
ba) on a commercial basis,
bb) as a service provider, its officer or employee defined in the act on the prevention and combating of money laundering and terrorist financing, in connection with the service provider's activity, or
bc) as an official person.
(8) Anyone who commits preparatory acts for money laundering is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding one year for a misdemeanor.
(9) The instigator or accomplice is not punishable if he commits the offense defined in subsection (3) or (4) with respect to property derived from a punishable act committed by him.

## 400. §

* (1) Anyone who conceals, transforms, transfers, participates in the alienation of, uses, conducts financial activity in connection with, uses financial services for, or disposes of property derived from a punishable act committed by another person, and does not know about the origin of the property due to negligence, is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding three years for a misdemeanor if the offense defined in subsection (1) is committed
a) for a particularly large value or higher,
b) as a service provider, its officer or employee defined in the act on the prevention and combating of money laundering and terrorist financing, in connection with the service provider's activity, or
c) as an official person.
(3) Anyone who makes a voluntary report to the authorities and discloses the circumstances of the commission, provided that the offense has not yet been discovered or has only been partially discovered, is not punishable for money laundering defined in subsections (1) and (2).

## 401. §

Anyone who fails to fulfill his reporting obligation prescribed by law in connection with the prevention and combating of money laundering and terrorist financing is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor.

## 402. § (1) *

(2) * For the purposes of §§ 399–400, financial activity and the use of financial services shall mean financial service or ancillary financial service, investment service or service ancillary to investment service, commodity exchange service, investment fund management, venture capital fund management, stock exchange, central securities depository or central counterparty, or insurance, reinsurance or independent insurance intermediary, or voluntary mutual insurance fund, private pension fund or occupational pension service activity, or the use thereof.

## 403. §

(1) Anyone who violates the documentary order prescribed in the act on accounting or in legislation based on its authorization, or breaches his bookkeeping or reporting obligation, and thereby
a) * causes an error materially affecting the reliable and true view, or
b) frustrates the review or control of his financial position for the given business year,
is punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.
(2) The individual entrepreneur and other economic operators not subject to the act on accounting who breach their registration and documentation obligations prescribed by law and thereby frustrate the review or control of their financial position are also punishable pursuant to subsection (1).
(3) * The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years in the case of subsection (1) if the offense is committed in the context of a financial institution, investment firm, commodity exchange service provider, investment fund manager, venture capital fund manager, organization conducting stock exchange, central securities depository or central counterparty activity, insurer, reinsurer or independent insurance intermediary, voluntary mutual insurance fund, private pension fund or occupational pension provider institution, or regulated real estate investment company.
(4) * For the purposes of this §, an error materially affects the reliable and true view if the combined value of errors and error effects – increasing or decreasing profit or equity – identified for a given business year, irrespective of sign, exceeds both twenty percent of the net sales revenue and twenty percent of the balance sheet total shown in the accounting report for the business year in which the error was committed. In all cases, an error materially affects the reliable and true view if the combined value of errors and error effects – increasing or decreasing profit or equity – identified for a given business year, irrespective of sign, exceeds five hundred million forints.

## 404. §

(1) Anyone who, in the case of an economic organisation subject to the Act on Bankruptcy Proceedings and Liquidation Proceedings, when threatened by insolvency,
a) by hiding, concealing, damaging, destroying, rendering unusable the assets or a part thereof,
b) by concluding a simulated transaction or acknowledging a dubious claim, or
c) in another manner contrary to the requirements of reasonable management
actually or ostensibly reduces the assets of the economic organisation, and thereby frustrates, in part or in whole, the satisfaction of the creditor or creditors, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.  
(2) Anyone shall be punishable according to paragraph (1) who, in respect of an economic organisation subject to the Act on Bankruptcy Proceedings and Liquidation Proceedings,
a) causes its insolvency or the appearance thereof by any of the conducts specified in paragraph (1), and thereby, or
b) in the case of its insolvency, by any of the conducts specified in paragraph (1),
frustrates, in part or in whole, the satisfaction of the creditor or creditors.  
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if
a) * the bankruptcy offence is committed in respect of an economic organisation subject to bankruptcy proceedings or liquidation proceedings of strategic importance, or
b) the extent of the actual or ostensible reduction of assets is particularly significant.  
(4) Anyone who, following the ordering of liquidation, gives preference to any of its creditors in violation of the order of satisfaction specified in the Act on Bankruptcy Proceedings and Liquidation Proceedings, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(5) The offence specified in paragraphs (1)–(3) is punishable if
a) the bankruptcy proceedings have been initiated,
b) the liquidation, compulsory deletion or compulsory winding-up proceedings have been ordered, or
c) the initiation of liquidation proceedings has not taken place contrary to the mandatory provision of law.  
(6) The bankruptcy offence may be committed as a principal by anyone who is entitled to dispose of the assets of the debtor economic organisation or a part thereof, or has the possibility to do so, even if the legal transaction serving as the basis for the disposal of the assets is invalid.  
404/A. § * A liquidator who, during the liquidation proceedings, obtains credible knowledge of the commission of a violation of the accounting rules (§ 403) or a bankruptcy offence (§ 404), and does not report this to the authority as soon as possible, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

## 405. §

(1) Anyone who withdraws, in part or in whole, assets serving as security for a claim based on a written contract, and thereby frustrates, in part or in whole, the settlement of the debt, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(2) Anyone who commits the act specified in paragraph (1) in respect of assets serving as security for a debt arising from economic activity shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one to five years if the offence specified in paragraph (2) is committed for a particularly large value or exceeding it.  
(4) * The perpetrator shall not be punishable for withdrawal of security for a debt if the debt is settled before the bringing of charges.

## 406. §

Anyone who brings goods into the territory of the country or takes them out without the permit necessary for their import or export shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 407. §

An executive officer or member of a public limited company, a limited liability company, a cooperative, a European public limited company or a European cooperative who withdraws, in part or in whole, the company's own capital shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 408. §

* (1) * Anyone who, without a permit prescribed by law or by a directly applicable legal act of the European Union, carries out
a) financial services or ancillary financial services,
b) investment services or services ancillary to investment services, commodity exchange services, investment fund management, venture capital fund management, stock exchange, central securities depository or central counterparty,
c) insurance, reinsurance,
d) voluntary mutual insurance fund, private pension fund, occupational pension provision,
e) crowdfunding services
activity, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(1a) * Anyone shall be punishable according to paragraph (1) who carries out pan-European personal pension product service or distribution activity without registration in the central register maintained by the European Insurance and Occupational Pensions Authority.  
(2) Anyone who, in connection with any of the activities specified in paragraph (1) points a)–c),
a) carries out intermediary activity without a permit prescribed by law,
b) * carries out intermediary activity or electronic money distribution activity in the absence of registration or notification in such a manner that it failed to verify that it has taken place,
shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

## 409. §

(1) A person in a leading position of an economic organisation who participates in ensuring that
a) the economic organisation cannot be found at its registered office, place of business or branch,
b) a person whose place of residence or place of stay is unknown or is deemed to be unknown is entered in a public register as a person authorised to represent the economic organisation, or
c) a person or economic organisation whose place of residence or place of stay is unknown or is deemed to be unknown, or which cannot be found at its registered office, place of business or branch, or who or which is not the beneficial owner, is entered in a public register as the owner of the economic organisation,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) Anyone who fails to notify data, right or fact to be entered in a public register, relating to economic activity, or fails to notify a change in such data, right or fact, if the notification obligation is prescribed by law, shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(3) For the purposes of this §, a beneficial owner means a person or economic organisation who or which holds, directly or indirectly, at least ten per cent of the voting rights or ownership share in the economic organisation, or has a determining influence within the meaning of the Civil Code.

## 410. § * (1) Whoever

a) by using inside information, concludes a transaction in their own name or in the name of another person in respect of a financial instrument affected by the inside information, gives an order to conclude a transaction, withdraws or modifies an order, records, withdraws or modifies an offer,
b) in view of the inside information in their possession, calls upon or induces another person or other persons to conclude a transaction in respect of a financial instrument affected by the inside information, to give, withdraw or modify an order to conclude a transaction, or to record, withdraw or modify an offer,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment not exceeding five years if
a) the criminal offence is committed as an official person,
b) the criminal offence is committed as an officer or employee of a financial institution, investment firm, commodity exchange service provider, investment fund manager, venture capital fund manager, organisation carrying out stock exchange, central securities depository or central counterparty activities, insurer, reinsurer or independent insurance intermediary, voluntary mutual insurance fund, private pension fund, occupational pension institution, or regulated real estate investment company,
c) * the criminal offence is committed as an officer or employee of a publicly offered securities issuer who has access to the inside information by virtue of their duties,
d) * the absolute value of the transaction or transactions, order or orders, offer or offers affected by the criminal offence is particularly large or exceeds that value.  
410/A. § * Whoever discloses inside information to an unauthorised person or persons in order to obtain an unlawful advantage or cause an unlawful disadvantage shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

## 411. § * (1) Whoever

a) * concludes a transaction, gives, withdraws or modifies an order, records, withdraws or modifies an offer in the course of dealing on own account, or engages in other conduct which gives false or misleading signals concerning the supply, demand, price or quotation of the given financial instrument or related spot commodity contract, or secures it at an artificial or abnormal level,
b) seeks to influence the price or quotation of a financial instrument or related spot commodity contract by concluding a fictitious transaction in respect of a financial instrument or by other deceptive conduct,
c) seeks to influence the calculation of a benchmark by communicating or transmitting false or misleading information, or by applying a deceptive method,
shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) Whoever, for the purpose of gaining profit, communicates or spreads information before the general public which gives false or misleading signals concerning the supply, demand, price or quotation of the given financial instrument or related spot commodity contract, or secures it at an artificial or abnormal level, shall be punishable in accordance with paragraph (1).  
(3) Whoever induces others to make a capital investment or increase an investment, or to sell a capital investment or reduce an investment, by communicating or spreading false data concerning the financial position of an economic organisation or a person in a leading position in connection with their activities, or concerning a financial instrument relating to the economic organisation, or by concealing data, shall also be punishable in accordance with paragraph (1).  
(4) * Whoever commits the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1)–(3)
a) in a criminal organisation,
b) as an official person,
c) as an officer or employee of a financial institution, investment firm, commodity exchange service provider, investment fund manager, venture capital fund manager, organisation carrying out stock exchange, central securities depository or central counterparty activities, insurer, reinsurer or independent insurance intermediary, voluntary mutual insurance fund, private pension fund, occupational pension institution, or regulated real estate investment company, or
d) as an officer or employee of a publicly offered securities issuer
shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding five years.  
(5) Paragraphs (1)–(4) shall also apply to transactions and instruments defined in Article 2(2)(a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 596/2014 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 16 April 2014 on market abuse (market abuse regulation) and repealing Directive 2003/6/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council and Commission Directives 2003/124/EC, 2003/125/EC and 2004/72/EC (hereinafter: Regulation (EU) No 596/2014), if the transaction, order, offer or conduct has an effect on the price, quotation or value of the financial instrument or spot commodity contract.

## 412. §

Whoever organises a game based on the collection and distribution of other people's money in a predetermined form and in a manner also containing a risk factor, in which participants joining in a chain-like manner make a payment or provide another service directly, or through the organiser, to participants ahead of them in the chain, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 413. §

(1) A person obliged to maintain banking, securities, fund, insurance or occupational pension secrecy who makes data qualifying as banking, securities, fund, insurance or occupational pension secrecy accessible to an unauthorised person for the purpose of obtaining an unlawful advantage or causing financial disadvantage to another person shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding two years.  
(2) The violation of business secrecy shall not be committed by a person who
a) fulfils their obligation defined in the Act on the publicity of data of public interest and data public on grounds of public interest, or
b) fulfils or initiates their reporting obligation prescribed by law in connection with the prevention and combating of money laundering and terrorist financing, insider dealing, market manipulation and terrorism, even if the report made by them in good faith was unfounded.

## 414. §

(1) * For the purposes of Sections 410–411, financial instrument shall mean a financial instrument defined in the Act on investment firms and commodity exchange service providers, and on the rules of the activities that may be carried out by them, provided that it complies with the provisions of Article 2(1) of Regulation (EU) No 596/2014, excluding
a) the cases defined in Article 5(1)–(3) of Regulation (EU) No 596/2014;  
b) the cases defined in Article 5(4) and (5) of Regulation (EU) No 596/2014;  
c) the cases defined in Article 6(1)–(4) of Regulation (EU) No 596/2014.  
(2) For the purposes of Sections 409 and 411, person in a leading position shall mean:
a) an executive officer of the economic organisation, a member of its supervisory board,
b) a person appointed to manage the Hungarian branch of an undertaking with a foreign seat, or the Hungarian place of business of a European Economic Interest Grouping with a foreign seat,
c) any person defined as such by the articles of association, deed of foundation or articles of partnership of the economic organisation.

## 415. §

(1) A person who places a product of poor quality on the market as a product of good quality shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years if the criminal offence is committed
a) in respect of a product of poor quality in significant quantity or value,
b) in a criminal organisation, or
c) as a business
.  
(3) A person who commits preparation for placing a product of poor quality on the market shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(4) A person who commits the criminal offence by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.  
(5) The perpetrator of the criminal offence defined in paragraph (4) shall not be punishable if, as soon as he becomes aware of the poor quality of the product, he does everything to ensure that the product of poor quality is returned to his possession.  
(6) * For the purposes of this Section, a product shall be of poor quality
a) if it does not meet the safety or quality requirements laid down in legislation or in a directly applicable legal act of general application of the European Union, or
b) in the absence of the provision referred to in point (a), if
ba) the unsuitability for human consumption of a product manufactured for the purpose of human consumption has been concealed,
bb) the best-before date, use-by date or expiry date of the product has been unlawfully extended,
bc) the product has been manufactured using a non-permitted ingredient,
bd) the product has been manufactured from materials or products unfit for human consumption for the purpose of human consumption, or
be) the product cannot be used for its intended purpose or its usability has been significantly reduced.

## 416. §

(1) A person who certifies false information about the conformity of a product in significant quantity or value in a certificate of conformity or declaration of conformity to be issued in the case of a conformity assessment obligation laid down in legislation or in a directly applicable legal act of the European Union, or by using a conformity marking, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.  
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one year to five years if the criminal offence is committed
a) in a criminal organisation, or
b) as a business
.  
(3) A person who commits the criminal offence by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

## 417. §

(1) A person who provides information capable of deception about the existence of a special price reduction or price advantage, or about the chance of winning at an organised product presentation shall be punishable for a misdemeanour by imprisonment not exceeding one year.
